# Hero art briefs

Generated by `tools/vnext/build_hero_art_briefs.py` from `data/vnext/catalog.json` and `production/hero-art/hero_art.json`. Do not edit by hand.

Follow [the visual standard](VISUAL_STANDARD.md). Every set has four views: `main.png`, `left.png`, `back.png`, `right.png`. Save every image exactly at the path shown.

## 02. Boar Rusher — `wc_vn_boar_rusher`

Orc Fighter · cost 1 · in the game

**Folder:** `art-source/asset-studio/wc_vn_boar_rusher/inputs/references/four_view_r001/`

| Colour | Hex |
|---|---|
| moss green skin | `#6E9A4B` |
| dark brown leather | `#5A3E2B` |
| bone white | `#E8E0CC` |

### `full/` — Complete hero with every held, strapped and floating item

**`full/main.png`**

```text
Painterly semi-realistic fantasy game art in the same style as the Shieldbearer reference: believable worn materials (steel plate with rivets and scratches, chainmail, stitched leather, weathered cloth with trim, bone, fur, scales), rendered like a clean 3D character turnaround. Strong readable silhouette, medium-sized detail and a limited dominant palette. Not a cartoon, not chibi, not a toy. Straight-on orthographic-style camera, even soft studio light, plain light-grey background #E6E6E6, no ground shadow, no scenery, no effects, no text. The whole subject is inside the frame with margin on every side. Full body, standing straight in a relaxed A-pose with the arms slightly away from the body and the legs slightly apart, looking straight ahead. Expression: a calm, serious expression. Design: Boar Rusher, an orc fighter. Big-shouldered orc with tusks, a boar-skull helm and a heavy club. Orc look: big and heavy with green skin, a heavy brow and two lower tusks; tribal gear of hide, fur, bone and stone. About 6.0 heads tall; huge shoulders and arms, deep bare chest, narrower waist, thick neck. Dominant colours: moss green skin #6E9A4B, dark brown leather #5A3E2B, bone white #E8E0CC; neutral leather, metal and skin tones are allowed beside them. Features: weathered, cracked boar-skull helmet worn over the top of his head, its two large curved tusks sweeping up and forward, tied under the chin with leather lashings; scarred orc face with a heavy brow and two short lower tusks from his own jaw; coarse dark-brown boar-fur mantle across both shoulders, held by a leather chest strap with an iron ring; wide leather belt with a bone buckle, knee-length kilt of stitched hide panels, leather wraps on the forearms and shins, bare feet. Right hand: heavy gnarled wooden club as long as his arm, bound with two iron bands and set with three large bone studs, held head-down at his right side, clear of the leg. Left hand: empty clenched fist. Seen from behind: the fur mantle covers the upper back; the rear of the boar skull and its lashings; the back panel of the kilt. FRONT VIEW. The subject faces the viewer directly.
```

## 03. Grove Druid — `wc_vn_grove_druid`

Elf Priest · cost 2 · in the game

**Folder:** `art-source/asset-studio/wc_vn_grove_druid/inputs/references/four_view_r001/`

| Colour | Hex |
|---|---|
| leaf green | `#5E9E55` |

### `full/` — Complete hero with every held, strapped and floating item

**`full/main.png`**

```text
Painterly semi-realistic fantasy game art in the same style as the Shieldbearer reference: believable worn materials (steel plate with rivets and scratches, chainmail, stitched leather, weathered cloth with trim, bone, fur, scales), rendered like a clean 3D character turnaround. Strong readable silhouette, medium-sized detail and a limited dominant palette. Not a cartoon, not chibi, not a toy. Straight-on orthographic-style camera, even soft studio light, plain light-grey background #E6E6E6, no ground shadow, no scenery, no effects, no text. The whole subject is inside the frame with margin on every side. Full body, standing straight in a relaxed A-pose with the arms slightly away from the body and the legs slightly apart, looking straight ahead. Expression: a calm, serious expression. Design: Grove Druid, an elf priest. Antlered elf elder in a leaf mantle with a twisted wooden staff. Elf look: tall and slender with long pointed ears swept back; elegant gear with leaf, bark and silver motifs. About 5.5 heads tall; slim elderly elf woman, slightly stooped, robe to the ground. Dominant colours: leaf green #5E9E55, bark brown #7A5A3A, cream #F0E6C8; neutral leather, metal and skin tones are allowed beside them. Features: kind lined face, long pointed elf ears and a long cream-white braid hanging over her right shoulder; pair of small branching antlers growing from her brow, wound with a thin vine; mantle of large overlapping green leaves across the shoulders down to the elbows, pinned with a wooden toggle; cream linen robe with a woven bark-brown belt and a green embroidered hem; feet hidden by the robe. Right hand: staff of twisted living wood taller than she is, standing upright on the ground at her right side; the top curls into a spiral that cradles one softly glowing green seed. Left hand: empty, open palm. Seen from behind: layers of the leaf mantle across the upper back, the plain robe below, the antlers from behind. FRONT VIEW. The subject faces the viewer directly.
```

Left hand: empty, open palm. Seen from behind: layers of the leaf mantle across the upper back, the plain robe below, the antlers from behind. RIGHT SIDE VIEW. The camera looks at the subject's own right side; it faces the right edge of the image, in exact profile.

## 04. Hookjaw — `wc_vn_hookjaw`

### `full/` — Complete hero with every held, strapped and floating item

**`full/main.png`**

```text
Painterly semi-realistic fantasy game art in the same style as the Shieldbearer reference: believable worn materials (steel plate with rivets and scratches, chainmail, stitched leather, weathered cloth with trim, bone, fur, scales), rendered like a clean 3D character turnaround. Strong readable silhouette, medium-sized detail and a limited dominant palette. Not a cartoon, not chibi, not a toy. Straight-on orthographic-style camera, even soft studio light, plain light-grey background #E6E6E6, no ground shadow, no scenery, no effects, no text. The whole subject is inside the frame with margin on every side. Full body, standing straight in a relaxed A-pose with the arms slightly away from the body and the legs slightly apart, looking straight ahead. Expression: a calm, serious expression. Design: Hookjaw, a goblin assassin. Wiry goblin with a metal jaw-guard and a hooked chain. Goblin look: small and wiry with yellow-green skin, very large pointed ears, a long nose and sharp teeth; scavenged, patched gear and bare feet. About 4.5 heads tall; small wiry goblin, long arms, slightly bent knees. Dominant colours: goblin green skin #8DB84A, rust red cloth #B5472E, dull iron #6F747A; neutral leather, metal and skin tones are allowed beside them. Features: big head with very large pointed, notched ears and narrow yellow eyes; bolted dull-iron jaw-guard covering his chin and mouth, riveted and scratched, held by leather straps around the head; rust-red hood worn up with the ears through slits, and a ragged red scarf; patched leather jerkin, dark trousers with knee patches, cloth-wrapped bare feet. Right hand: large iron hook the size of his head, held by its shank at his right side, its heavy chain wound in three loops around the right forearm. Left hand: empty, clawed fingers. Seen from behind: the back of the hood and the scarf tails, the jaw-guard straps, the jerkin's patched back. FRONT VIEW. The subject faces the viewer directly.
```

**`full/main.png`**

```text
Painterly semi-realistic fantasy game art in the same style as the Shieldbearer reference: believable worn materials (steel plate with rivets and scratches, chainmail, stitched leather, weathered cloth with trim, bone, fur, scales), rendered like a clean 3D character turnaround. Full body, standing straight in a relaxed A-pose with the arms slightly away from the body and the legs slightly apart, looking straight ahead. Expression: a calm, serious expression. Design: Chapel Healer, a human priest. Robed cleric with a tall hood and a large lantern staff. Human look: natural human skin tones; practical medieval kingdom gear of steel, wool and leather with brass fittings. About 5.5 heads tall; medium woman, soft rounded shape, robe to the ground. Dominant colours: white robe #F4F1E8, sky blue trim #6FB1E3, warm gold #E3B94A; neutral leather, metal and skin tones are allowed beside them. Features: tall pointed white hood with a sky-blue band; warm, kind round face, hair hidden; layered white wool robe to the ground with a slightly frayed hem; wide sky-blue stole over both shoulders hanging to the knees, a gold embroidered sun at each end; leather belt with a small book pouch. Right hand: tall wooden staff standing upright on the ground at her right side, topped with a large round iron-and-glass lantern that glows warm gold. Left hand: empty, open palm. Seen from behind: the hood's point and back, the stole crossing behind the neck, the plain robe. FRONT VIEW. The subject faces the viewer directly.
```

**`full/main.png`**

```text
Painterly semi-realistic fantasy game art in the same style as the Shieldbearer reference: believable worn materials (steel plate with rivets and scratches, chainmail, stitched leather, weathered cloth with trim, bone, fur, scales), rendered like a clean 3D character turnaround. Design: Hammerer, a dwarf fighter. Short broad dwarf with a braided beard and a huge two-handed hammer. Dwarf look: short and very broad with big hands, a big nose and a heavy beard; chunky riveted iron, copper and brass gear. About 4.5 heads tall; short and very wide, huge hands. Dominant colours: burnt orange beard #D9762B, iron grey #70777F, dark red cloth #8E2F2A; neutral leather, metal and skin tones are allowed beside them. Features: huge burnt-orange beard in two thick braids bound with iron rings, reaching the belt; big nose and heavy brows; round dented iron helmet with a nose guard and a riveted rim; dark-red quilted tunic with short chainmail sleeves, a thick leather belt with a square iron buckle, leather bracers; heavy iron-capped boots. Right hand: war hammer with a block-shaped iron head as big as his chest and a long leather-wrapped oak haft, held upright at his right side with the head resting on the ground beside his right foot. Left hand: empty fist. Seen from behind: the helmet's back with its rim, the quilted tunic and belt. FRONT VIEW. The subject faces the viewer directly.
```

Right hand: stone gauntlet fist, hanging at his side. Left hand: matching stone gauntlet fist. Seen from behind: the harness crossing the broad back, the stone shoulder slab from behind. Goblin look: small and wiry with yellow-green skin, very large pointed ears, a long nose and sharp teeth; scavenged, patched gear and bare feet. About 4.0 heads tall; tiny, round belly, thin limbs. ### `full/` — Complete hero with every held, strapped and floating item

**`full/main.png`**

```text
Painterly semi-realistic fantasy game art in the same style as the Shieldbearer reference: believable worn materials (steel plate with rivets and scratches, chainmail, stitched leather, weathered cloth with trim, bone, fur, scales), rendered like a clean 3D character turnaround. Strong readable silhouette, medium-sized detail and a limited dominant palette. Not a cartoon, not chibi, not a toy. Straight-on orthographic-style camera, even soft studio light, plain light-grey background #E6E6E6, no ground shadow, no scenery, no effects, no text. The whole subject is inside the frame with margin on every side. Full body, standing straight in a relaxed A-pose with the arms slightly away from the body and the legs slightly apart, looking straight ahead. Expression: a calm, serious expression. Design: Blood Drummer, an orc shaman. Heavy orc with painted arms beating a large waist drum. Orc look: big and heavy with green skin, a heavy brow and two lower tusks; tribal gear of hide, fur, bone and stone. About 6.0 heads tall; heavy, big belly and thick arms, bare torso. Dominant colours: olive green skin #6F8F4A, blood red paint #B5312C, hide tan #C9A878; neutral leather, metal and skin tones are allowed beside them. Features: two short tusks and black hair in a topknot bound with a bone ring; blood-red war-paint stripes on both arms and across the face; leather shoulder strap, bone-bead necklace, hide kilt, leg wraps, bare feet; large round war drum with a wooden shell, tan hide skin and rope tensioning, strapped at his waist in front with the skin facing forward. Right hand: thick drumstick with a round leather-wrapped head, held at his right side. Left hand: matching drumstick held at his left side. Seen from behind: the drum's strap crossing the back, the topknot, the kilt's back. FRONT VIEW. The subject faces the viewer directly.
```

### `full/` — Complete hero with every held, strapped and floating item

**`full/main.png`**

```text
Painterly semi-realistic fantasy game art in the same style as the Shieldbearer reference: believable worn materials (steel plate with rivets and scratches, chainmail, stitched leather, weathered cloth with trim, bone, fur, scales), rendered like a clean 3D character turnaround. Strong readable silhouette, medium-sized detail and a limited dominant palette. Not a cartoon, not chibi, not a toy. Straight-on orthographic-style camera, even soft studio light, plain light-grey background #E6E6E6, no ground shadow, no scenery, no effects, no text. The whole subject is inside the frame with margin on every side. Full body, standing straight in a relaxed A-pose with the arms slightly away from the body and the legs slightly apart, looking straight ahead. Expression: a calm, serious expression. Design: Moonblade, an elf assassin. Lean masked elf with two crescent blades and a short cape. Elf look: tall and slender with long pointed ears swept back; elegant gear with leaf, bark and silver motifs. About 5.5 heads tall; lean, narrow and athletic. Dominant colours: midnight blue #27325E, silver #C9D2DC, pale lilac skin #C9B8E0; neutral leather, metal and skin tones are allowed beside them. Features: smooth silver half-mask covering the eyes and nose with a crescent engraved on the brow; pale lilac skin, long pointed ears, white hair tied in a short tail; short midnight-blue cape reaching the waist, fastened with a silver crescent clasp; fitted dark-blue leather armour with silver trim, tightly wrapped sleeves, a cloth sash and soft boots. Right hand: crescent-shaped silver blade with a wrapped grip, held pointing down at the right side. Left hand: matching crescent blade held pointing down at the left side. Seen from behind: the short cape to the waist, the hair tail over it, the sash knot at the back. FRONT VIEW. The subject faces the viewer directly.
```

### `full/` — Complete hero with every held, strapped and floating item

**`full/main.png`**

```text
Painterly semi-realistic fantasy game art in the same style as the Shieldbearer reference: believable worn materials (steel plate with rivets and scratches, chainmail, stitched leather, weathered cloth with trim, bone, fur, scales), rendered like a clean 3D character turnaround. Full body, standing straight in a relaxed A-pose with the arms slightly away from the body and the legs slightly apart, looking straight ahead. Expression: a calm, serious expression. Design: Ironwall, a dwarf tank. Squat dwarf sealed in thick armour with two bolted shields. Dwarf look: short and very broad with big hands, a big nose and a heavy beard; chunky riveted iron, copper and brass gear. About 4.5 heads tall; squat, almost square, fully armoured. Dominant colours: dark steel #5C6670, copper #C27B4A, cream beard #EDE3CC; neutral leather, metal and skin tones are allowed beside them. Features: closed riveted barrel helmet with a narrow eye slit; only a short cream beard shows below it; thick dark-steel plate armour with large copper rivets and copper trim, layered pauldrons and chainmail at the joints; wide flat sabatons. Right hand: round-topped bolted steel shield as tall as his chest with copper rivets, held at his right side, face pointing forward, clear of the body. Left hand: identical bolted shield held at his left side, face pointing forward. Seen from behind: the plain back plate with copper rivets; the inner sides of both shields with their grips. FRONT VIEW. The subject faces the viewer directly.
```

Features: the forge body is a rounded furnace of riveted dark iron plates with a glowing orange grate as its face and a short chimney pipe at the back; two short thick piston legs with wide flat iron feet; no arms; one rocket rack holding four red-tipped rockets on each shoulder of the forge; a dwarf with a long white beard, a plain gold crown and a fur-collared coat sits in an open seat on top. Right hand: on a brass control lever. Left hand: on a brass control lever. Seen from behind: the chimney pipe, the back of the seat and the rider's coat, the rear of both rocket racks. FRONT VIEW. The subject faces the viewer directly.

### `full/` — Complete hero with every held, strapped and floating item

**`full/main.png`**

```text
Painterly semi-realistic fantasy game art in the same style as the Shieldbearer reference: believable worn materials (steel plate with rivets and scratches, chainmail, stitched leather, weathered cloth with trim, bone, fur, scales), rendered like a clean 3D character turnaround. Strong readable silhouette, medium-sized detail and a limited dominant palette. Not a cartoon, not chibi, not a toy. Straight-on orthographic-style camera, even soft studio light, plain light-grey background #E6E6E6, no ground shadow, no scenery, no effects, no text. The whole subject is inside the frame with margin on every side. Full body, standing straight in a relaxed A-pose with the arms slightly away from the body and the legs slightly apart, looking straight ahead. Expression: a calm, serious expression. Design: Jinxer, a goblin shaman. Hunched goblin in a feathered mask shaking a bone rattle. Goblin look: small and wiry with yellow-green skin, very large pointed ears, a long nose and sharp teeth; scavenged, patched gear and bare feet. About 4.5 heads tall; small, hunched, long arms. Dominant colours: olive green skin #7C9A45, purple #7B4BB0, bone white #E9E2CF; neutral leather, metal and skin tones are allowed beside them. Features: carved wooden mask with two round eye holes and white painted streaks, topped with three large purple feathers; big ears stick out at the sides; purple woven shawl with a frayed edge and a necklace of five large teeth; hide loincloth, bare feet with ankle beads. Right hand: bone rattle: a long bone handle with a round dried-gourd head and two small feathers tied below it. Left hand: empty, clawed fingers spread. Seen from behind: the mask's cord tied behind the head, the feathers from behind, the shawl's back. FRONT VIEW. The subject faces the viewer directly.
```

### `full/` — Complete hero with every held, strapped and floating item

**`full/main.png`**

```text
Painterly semi-realistic fantasy game art in the same style as the Shieldbearer reference: believable worn materials (steel plate with rivets and scratches, chainmail, stitched leather, weathered cloth with trim, bone, fur, scales), rendered like a clean 3D character turnaround. Strong readable silhouette, medium-sized detail and a limited dominant palette. Not a cartoon, not chibi, not a toy. Straight-on orthographic-style camera, even soft studio light, plain light-grey background #E6E6E6, no ground shadow, no scenery, no effects, no text. The whole subject is inside the frame with margin on every side. Full body, standing straight in a relaxed A-pose with the arms slightly away from the body and the legs slightly apart, looking straight ahead. Expression: a calm, serious expression. Design: Bonechanter, an orc warlock. Tall orc in a bone headdress with a skull-topped staff. Orc look: big and heavy with green skin, a heavy brow and two lower tusks; tribal gear of hide, fur, bone and stone. About 6.0 heads tall; tall, lean for an orc, straight back. Dominant colours: dusty green skin #7E9665, bone white #ECE5D2, dark crimson #7A1F2B; neutral leather, metal and skin tones are allowed beside them. Features: headdress made of a large horned animal skull worn over the head, with three long dark feathers behind it; lean face with small tusks; dark-crimson robe to the ankles, open at the chest, with a rib-bone chest piece laced on with leather; bone bracelets and a leather belt hung with small bone charms; bare feet. Right hand: tall wooden staff standing upright at his right side, topped with a small skull whose eye sockets glow red. Left hand: empty, raised slightly. Seen from behind: the skull headdress and its three feathers from behind, the plain back of the robe. FRONT VIEW. The subject faces the viewer directly.
```

**`full/main.png`**

```text
Painterly semi-realistic fantasy game art in the same style as the Shieldbearer reference: believable worn materials (steel plate with rivets and scratches, chainmail, stitched leather, weathered cloth with trim, bone, fur, scales), rendered like a clean 3D character turnaround. The whole subject is inside the frame with margin on every side. Full body, standing straight in a relaxed A-pose with the arms slightly away from the body and the legs slightly apart, looking straight ahead. Expression: a calm, serious expression. Design: Foxclaw, a beastkin assassin. Small fox-headed fighter with a big tail and two claws. Beastkin look: upright humanoid body covered in fur or feathers with a full animal head and clawed hands; simple tribal leather and cloth. About 5.5 heads tall; small, light, digitigrade legs. Dominant colours: fox orange fur #E2792F, cream fur #F5E9D2, dark teal cloth #2F6F73; neutral leather, metal and skin tones are allowed beside them. Features: fox head with large upright black-tipped ears, a cream muzzle and amber eyes; orange fur over the body with a cream chest and dark fur on the forearms and lower legs; very large bushy tail with a cream tip; dark-teal sash around the waist, teal arm wraps and a leather chest strap. Right hand: three-bladed steel claw on a leather bracer strapped over the back of the hand. Left hand: matching three-bladed claw. Seen from behind: the big tail hangs behind the legs; the sash knot and the chest strap across the back. FRONT VIEW. The subject faces the viewer directly.
```

### `full/` — Complete hero with every held, strapped and floating item

**`full/main.png`**

```text
Painterly semi-realistic fantasy game art in the same style as the Shieldbearer reference: believable worn materials (steel plate with rivets and scratches, chainmail, stitched leather, weathered cloth with trim, bone, fur, scales), rendered like a clean 3D character turnaround. Strong readable silhouette, medium-sized detail and a limited dominant palette. Not a cartoon, not chibi, not a toy. Straight-on orthographic-style camera, even soft studio light, plain light-grey background #E6E6E6, no ground shadow, no scenery, no effects, no text. The whole subject is inside the frame with margin on every side. Full body, standing straight in a relaxed A-pose with the arms slightly away from the body and the legs slightly apart, looking straight ahead. Expression: a calm, serious expression. Design: Owl Seer, a beastkin shaman. Owl-headed sage in a feather cloak with a crooked staff. Beastkin look: upright humanoid body covered in fur or feathers with a full animal head and clawed hands; simple tribal leather and cloth. About 5.5 heads tall; rounded, short neck, cloak to the ground. Dominant colours: tawny brown feathers #9A6B3F, cream feathers #F2E6C9, indigo #3D3A8C; neutral leather, metal and skin tones are allowed beside them. Features: owl head with very large round yellow eyes, a cream facial disc, two ear tufts and a small hooked beak; cloak of large layered tawny feathers closed at the front and reaching the ground, cream feathers at the collar; small indigo scarf; taloned feet just visible at the hem. Right hand: feathered taloned hand emerging from the cloak, holding a crooked wooden staff with a hanging indigo charm, upright at the right side. Left hand: hidden inside the cloak. Seen from behind: rows of large layered feathers down the whole back, the ear tufts from behind. FRONT VIEW. The subject faces the viewer directly.
```

**`full/main.png`**

```text
Painterly semi-realistic fantasy game art in the same style as the Shieldbearer reference: believable worn materials (steel plate with rivets and scratches, chainmail, stitched leather, weathered cloth with trim, bone, fur, scales), rendered like a clean 3D character turnaround. Full body, standing straight in a relaxed A-pose with the arms slightly away from the body and the legs slightly apart, looking straight ahead. Expression: a calm, serious expression. Design: Hellblade, a demon fighter. Red horned warrior with one burning greatsword. Demon look: horned, with red or purple skin and glowing eyes; black iron gear and one glowing accent colour. About 6.0 heads tall; muscular, wide stance, bare chest. Dominant colours: crimson skin #B5302E, black iron #2B2A2E, flame orange #F58A1F; neutral leather, metal and skin tones are allowed beside them. Features: two short thick horns curving upward, yellow eyes and a sharp jaw; black iron shoulder guards, bracers and a wide belt; scarred bare chest; dark leather war-skirt with iron plates, iron greaves; short pointed tail. Right hand: greatsword as long as he is tall with a black iron blade, the cutting edge glowing flame orange with low flames along it, held upright at his right side with the point on the ground. Left hand: empty fist. Seen from behind: the bare back between the shoulder guards, the tail, the back of the war-skirt. FRONT VIEW. The subject faces the viewer directly.
```

### `full/` — Complete hero with every held, strapped and floating item

**`full/main.png`**

```text
Painterly semi-realistic fantasy game art in the same style as the Shieldbearer reference: believable worn materials (steel plate with rivets and scratches, chainmail, stitched leather, weathered cloth with trim, bone, fur, scales), rendered like a clean 3D character turnaround. Strong readable silhouette, medium-sized detail and a limited dominant palette. Not a cartoon, not chibi, not a toy. Straight-on orthographic-style camera, even soft studio light, plain light-grey background #E6E6E6, no ground shadow, no scenery, no effects, no text. The whole subject is inside the frame with margin on every side. Full body, standing straight in a relaxed A-pose with the arms slightly away from the body and the legs slightly apart, looking straight ahead. Expression: a calm, serious expression. Design: Curse Weaver, an undead warlock. Veiled ghoul with long sleeves and a floating curse doll. Undead look: bone, grey pallor and stitches; torn, faded cloth and a cold glow in the eyes. About 5.5 heads tall; thin woman in a robe whose ragged hem hides the feet. Dominant colours: pale grey skin #B9BDC2, plum robe #6A2F6B, sickly yellow #D8D455; neutral leather, metal and skin tones are allowed beside them. Features: black lace veil covering the eyes; only the pale mouth and chin are visible; plum robe with a ragged hem, sickly-yellow stitched trim and a rope belt; very long sleeves hanging past the hands. Right hand: pale fingers just visible at the sleeve, holding a long bone needle with a trailing yellow thread, pointing down. Left hand: hidden in the sleeve. Also: a stitched rag doll with button eyes and three pins floats beside her left shoulder, clearly separated from the body. Seen from behind: the veil tied behind the head over dark hair, the robe's plain back with yellow trim at the hem. FRONT VIEW. The subject faces the viewer directly.
```

### `full/` — Complete hero with every held, strapped and floating item

**`full/main.png`**

```text
Painterly semi-realistic fantasy game art in the same style as the Shieldbearer reference: believable worn materials (steel plate with rivets and scratches, chainmail, stitched leather, weathered cloth with trim, bone, fur, scales), rendered like a clean 3D character turnaround. Strong readable silhouette, medium-sized detail and a limited dominant palette. Not a cartoon, not chibi, not a toy. Straight-on orthographic-style camera, even soft studio light, plain light-grey background #E6E6E6, no ground shadow, no scenery, no effects, no text. The whole subject is inside the frame with margin on every side. Full body, standing straight in a relaxed A-pose with the arms slightly away from the body and the legs slightly apart, looking straight ahead. Expression: a calm, serious expression. Design: Stone Shaper, an elemental summoner. Boulder-bodied figure with glowing cracks and oversized fists. Elemental look: the body is made of the element itself, with no clothing, two simple glowing eyes and no mouth. About 5.5 heads tall; body built of five large rounded boulders, short thick stone legs. Dominant colours: warm grey stone #8D8A83, moss green #6E9A4B, glowing amber #F2A93B; neutral leather, metal and skin tones are allowed beside them. Features: weathered, chipped boulders with specks of lichen form the chest, shoulders and hips; the head is one rounded rock with two glowing amber eyes and no mouth; amber light glows in the cracks between the rocks; a patch of moss and a small tuft of grass on the left shoulder. Right hand: oversized rock fist. Left hand: oversized rock fist. Seen from behind: the boulders of the back with amber cracks between them; the moss on the left shoulder. FRONT VIEW. The subject faces the viewer directly.
```

Strong readable silhouette, medium-sized detail and a limited dominant palette. Not a cartoon, not chibi, not a toy. Straight-on orthographic-style camera, even soft studio light, plain light-grey background #E6E6E6, no ground shadow, no scenery, no effects, no text. The whole subject is inside the frame with margin on every side. Full body, standing straight in a relaxed A-pose with the arms slightly away from the body and the legs slightly apart, looking straight ahead. Expression: a calm, serious expression. Design: Void Wyrm, a dragonkin warlock. Dark-scaled dragonkin with hollow glowing chest and tattered wings. Dragonkin look: upright dragon-headed humanoid with scales, horns, a tail and clawed digitigrade feet; regal bearing. About 7.0 heads tall; lean, hunched, long neck. Dominant colours: near-black scales #24222E, void purple #6A3FB8, glowing blue #5FB8FF; neutral leather, metal and skin tones are allowed beside them. Features: dragon head with a narrow snout, two jagged horns and two glowing blue eyes; near-black scales with a void-purple sheen on the throat, wing membranes and tail tip; hollow round opening in the chest glowing blue; pair of tattered wings with torn edges, half open; long thin tail. Right hand: empty long claws. Left hand: empty long claws. Seen from behind: the tattered wings from behind, a ridge of jagged scales down the spine; the chest opening is not visible.
